# Milestone 01 — Draft v2.2 Release Inventory

> **Working inventory, not release notes or final feature approval.** 26 November 2026. Published baseline [v2.1](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/tree/v2.1) `c3237114108cad9f7d4e57657b6aed5e0091a5ef`, schema 10; unreleased integration [milestone-1 checkpoint](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/tree/fdc677920bfc52f48f641b3d6d1344c902a6a9b0) `fdc677920bfc52f48f641b3d6d1344c902a6a9b0`, schema 31. Authority: [#366 inventory requirement](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/issues/366#issuecomment-7185503954) and [approved work plan](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/issues/366#issuecomment-7196554739).

## Initial feature reconciliation

The following is a starting map from merged PR titles and existing status/evidence, not an exhaustive or independently verified list of individual changes. Expand each row into individual feature/fix entries with exact issue/PR, final source references, guide and screenshot references, applicable version, permissions, evidence and outstanding checks. Classify each entry as user-visible, security/data integrity, deployment, documentation or internal maintenance. Only verified, owner-approved material may feed final website and release notes; no website changes authorised.

| Area | Candidate delivered scope | References | Review state |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Accounts | Configurable IT Staff role, individual overrides, preserved previous grants on upgrade, MFA and account/session safeguards | [#358](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/pull/358), [#365](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/issues/365) | PR description reviewed; final source/guide reconciliation pending |
| Infrastructure | Rack search spacing; optional feature controls; independent Device addressing and explicit IPAM linking; Rack preservation | #359, #371–#376, #380 | Merge-title inventory; reconcile detailed changes |
| Staff and Departments | Directory and Department foundation/APIs, interface, assignments, My Equipment and Department Details | #377–#382, #386 | Reconcile with later #453, #465–#466, #470 |
| IT Assets | Optional module, intake, stock, issue/return/write-off, registration/reissue, associated equipment, prepared handover, correction, confirmation, overview and low-stock reporting | #387–#393, #398 | Detailed source/guide reconciliation pending |
| Safeguards and usability | VM hardware/category labels, essential audit access, stale Staff edits, Device identity, concurrent password changes, expired-session sign-in, help text | #399, #401–#403, #405–#406, #408 | Individual change reconciliation pending |
| Suppliers and lifecycle | Supplier management, Issues/Purchasing, equipment integration and lifecycle warnings | #414–#416, #418; issues #410–#413 | Individual change reconciliation pending |
| Service Desk | Ticket foundation, IT queue, My Tickets/Department Tickets, administration/reporting, templates/branding, notifications | #437–#442; issues #420–#425 | Reconcile shared-device privacy and later refinements |
| Stage 6 | Recovery confirmation, protected legacy import, shared-device handling, bounded HTTPS listen queue, unified Staff administration | #444–#445, #447, #451, #453 | Detail and evidence reconciliation pending |
| Stage 6.6 | Security, Staff administration and validation; My Equipment/unread replies/Audit Trail; visual consistency and dark mode | #465–#467 | Expand each approved issue and individual fix |
| Native corrections | Friendly known optional-feature audit labels; aligned profile fields; four-tab Manage Permissions with visible Done and retained edits; linked employment/login safeguards and upgrade reconciliation; Department refresh and custody history | [#470](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/pull/470), [evidence](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/blob/fdc677920bfc52f48f641b3d6d1344c902a6a9b0/docs/development-handovers/evidence/native-acceptance-corrections/README.md), [checkpoint](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/issues/351#issuecomment-7196414155) | Five targeted native Safari checks recorded as passed; wider acceptance pending |
| Verification/internal | Historical secret-scan handling, browser/CI fixes, local integrated regression and backup/restore rehearsal | #395, #417, #446, #448, #449, #469 | Not automatically new user-facing functionality; distinguish evidence from shipped change |

## Detailed candidate entries — Stages 1–3

26 November 2026. The following 65 individually tracked entries were derived from the merged PR descriptions and selected existing guide/contract text. They are candidates for v2.2 release notes, not final-source verification or owner publication approval. Earlier behaviour is qualified by subsequent changes; do not publish historical intermediate workflows. All entries apply to the unreleased v2.2 candidate relative to v2.1, with explicit upgrade caveats. Issue references are included where established by the PR; the PR links retain the original authority and evidence links. Final code-path mapping, individual guide review, screenshot approval and #443 verification remain outstanding. The labels and IDs are working inventory identifiers, not new product requirements.

| ID | Classification | Candidate change | Merged PR(s) | Guide / review destination | Qualification or follow-up |

### Stage 1–3 non-feature work and verification

- PR #375: Stage 2 persistence and integration verification, no application change. Do not count as a new feature.
- PR #383: draft guide/status work; #385: synchronisation of approved v2.1 documentation; #407: comprehensive guide expansion; #404: stop-and-simplify guidance; #409: permanent documentation standard. Documentation/process entries, not application functionality.
- PR #395: final-review CI sequencing; internal maintenance, not user functionality.
- PR #359 also changed a private acceptance build-helper branch allowlist; distinguish this from the visible Rack spacing fix.
- PR #362 has no recorded merge date in the milestone-targeted closed PR result despite a merge SHA; reconcile ancestry before attributing a CI change. PR #379 was a preview, not a delivered feature.
- Check earlier main-branch changes since the v2.1 tag and final tree differences; the milestone-targeted PR list is not an exhaustive commit history.

### Cross-stage documentation finding requiring correction

**Priority — incorrect employment security instruction.** At the approved schema-31 checkpoint, [Roles, Section Permissions And MFA](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/blob/fdc677920bfc52f48f641b3d6d1344c902a6a9b0/docs/how-tos/features-and-instructions/v2.2-draft/Roles-Section-Permissions-and-MFA.md) says: “Making Staff inactive does not disable its linked login”. This is superseded by merged [PR #470](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/pull/470) and the [native correction evidence](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/blob/fdc677920bfc52f48f641b3d6d1344c902a6a9b0/docs/development-handovers/evidence/native-acceptance-corrections/README.md): Left Company atomically disables the linked login and revokes sessions and proofs; reactivation leaves login disabled until an authorised separate enable. Staff status changes for linked accounts require administrator authority in addition to Staff Members Edit. Retain the distinction that disabling a login does not return equipment or change employment status. Correct during Batch 3, check every related guide for stale statements, and keep all acceptance gates. This inventory records the contradiction; no instructional page changed here.

All apply to the unreleased v2.2 candidate; this is not final-source or publication approval. Issue links are included where established; #470 authority is the [owner-approved native correction decision](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/issues/351#issuecomment-7189258357) and [checkpoint](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/issues/351#issuecomment-7196414155). Where related entries split a single change into user-facing details, consolidate for release prose rather than counting them as separate features. Earlier development states (including draft PR descriptions) are not the current merge or acceptance status. Final code-path mapping, precise labels, guide coverage, screenshot provenance and independent #443 review remain outstanding.

## Earlier main and nested merge reconciliation — 26 November 2026

The comparison [published v2.1 to Stage 1 merge](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/compare/c3237114108cad9f7d4e57657b6aed5e0091a5ef...6008fedfc2c25fc40b49c9e9e21f3a93d61a9f4a) returned all 72 commits. The first 100 closed main-targeted PR records include the release boundary and the later main merges shown below. No claim is made about all historical main PR pages. Main documentation changes may have been synchronised rather than merged by ancestry; distinguish source equivalence from merge topology.

| Record | Scope | Treatment |
| --- | --- | --- |
| #281, #336, #337 | v2.1 modular interface and final publication wording | At or before published tag; not v2.2 changes. |
| #338, #342, #343 | v2.1 README screenshots, release notes and interface wording | Published-version documentation maintenance, review in Batches 2/8. |
| #344 | v2.1 publication-status audit and guide compatibility copies | Published-version maintenance; preserve version accuracy. |
| #354 | Roadmap Milestones 01–03 alignment | Planning/reference maintenance. |
| #356, #357 | Simplified v2.1 installation quick starts and labelled example IPs | v2.1 documentation/configuration maintenance; Batch 6. |
| #358 | Stage 1 role/account implementation including earlier commits | Expanded S1-07–S1-14 to capture previously omitted detail; reviewed the complete 72-commit subject list, role contract, final role policy and exact d3c1e059 correction patch. |
| #384, #385 | Approved public v2.1 corrections and sync into milestone | Documentation maintenance, not newly released functionality. |
| #428 → #418 | UK date entry/calendar and display-only type labels | Nested feature-branch merge confirmed; issues #426/#427 added to S4-18/S4-19. Date scope includes four lifecycle and six stock workflows, strict calendar validation, eight-digit shortcut, min/max/required/readonly/disabled preservation, accessible keyboard/touch calendar; ISO fields and form participation retained. Read-only Lifecycle, Supplier/audit/rack-print timestamps use UK order. |

Stage 1 internal changes include enabling milestone CI (b13e5ce9), private build branch guards (53252613 and #359), verification and documentation. The original correction commit d3c1e059 and [owner checkpoint](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/issues/351#issuecomment-6805479377) establish S1-12 and S1-13; do not overlook these because the broad Stage 1 PR description emphasises IT Staff. The final role policy at [backend/role_policy.py](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/blob/fdc677920bfc52f48f641b3d6d1344c902a6a9b0/backend/role_policy.py) confirms enabled-role inheritance, explicit override precedence, protected baseline and wider MFA logic; final integration call paths and interface remain to be checked. Historical role guide wording is not final: later Staff administration and administrator-promotion rules supersede it.

### Cross-cutting version and wording decisions for later guide review

- Final [role policy](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/blob/fdc677920bfc52f48f641b3d6d1344c902a6a9b0/backend/role_policy.py#L4-L7) registers eight section permissions. Staff Members, Departments and Service Desk are core (not organisation-switch-controlled), whereas the six organisation switches are Devices, IPAM, Racks, IT Assets, Suppliers and Equipment Lifecycle. Lifecycle has no independent ninth section permission: review its existing Devices/administrator boundaries in Batch 4. Do not confuse feature switches with section grants. The current draft Roles guide still references the six-section historical editor and Batch 4.1 wording; reconcile against all eight current permissions.
- Role changes invalidate sessions/email proofs when indicated by membership/effective access/MFA changes, and also revoke password-change proofs when section state changes. Avoid implying all harmless role edits sign everyone out. Queue admission does not confirm email delivery.
- Existing approved offboarding correction in #470 remains the priority Batch 3 finding, not a new application issue. No code or instructional guide changed here.

### Follow-up on earlier source entries

- S2-06/S2-09: [Device creation](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/blob/fdc677920bfc52f48f641b3d6d1344c902a6a9b0/backend/device_saves.py#L290-L325) independently requires Devices Edit, uses Rack/IPAM preparation and requires IPAM Edit only for managed nonblank addresses. Rack preservation helper is also invoked during existing Device saves (line 173). Full UI and related save paths remain subject to guide review.
- S1-13/S2-04: [inventory projection](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/blob/fdc677920bfc52f48f641b3d6d1344c902a6a9b0/backend/inventory_projection.py#L10-L35) removes unpermitted Device collections; read-only Rack controls do not themselves grant mounted Device identity. Direct writes and related import/history boundaries remain outstanding.
- Staff profile reconciliation is directional for email: an existing Staff email does not silently populate a blank account address. Record exact conflict and verification behaviour in Batch 3, rather than claiming all blanks synchronise both ways.
- Initial physical allocation is currently limited to Desktop, Laptop and Printer. Do not advertise all physical Device types as eligible. Employee software list is recorded inventory, not a live scan.
- Offboarding source confirms the earlier priority documentation finding. Keep the actual approved Left Company label and separate login re-enabling instruction. No change to the application is proposed.

### Important distinctions for instructions and release copy

- Receipt creates physical stock; checked readiness changes availability, not held quantity. Registration of compatible unregistered Ready computer stock transfers one unit into a canonical held Device without altering the total held or Ready quantity. Only physical issue/reissue removes it from held stock. Direct handover changes custody, not held quantity or the physical issue chart.
- Do not conflate the initial allocation allowlist (Desktop, Laptop, Printer), computer stock registration allowlist (Desktop, Laptop, physical Server), and later eligible physical return/reissue. Shared multi-Department custody remains limited to eligible Desktop, Laptop and Printer.
- A minimum stock level is optional and only for quantity-based stock. Warning tests Ready To Issue strictly below the minimum; no warning when equal, and zero differs from unset. Dashboard current balances are distinct from movement totals within selected 30/90-day period.
- Correct Movement is a quantity correction, not a delete/edit history function. The current validation rejects zero, unchanged quantities, individual identity corrections, impossible later balances and reopening accessories after ended custody. Historical metadata/specification protection is present at schema level; do not advertise an unverified specification-correction form.
- Staff help source contains potentially ambiguous 'when Service Desk is enabled' wording. Service Desk is a core permission-controlled section, not a seventh organisation switch. Carry forward to guide review; no application copy change authorised or made.

### Important distinctions for Batch 4 instructions and website copy

- Purchasing concerns Suppliers and Supplier Issues, not the employee or IT Service Desk. Do not imply purchase-order functionality from the dashboard title.
- Keep the actual receipt, canonical Supplier association and historical supply relationship separate. Removal is individual only; a bulk assignment is not bulk removal. A stock-derived Device does not add a second supplied quantity.
- Equipment Lifecycle does not create another section permission. First physical issue can establish first-service history even while the optional display feature is disabled; dates are not inferred merely from existing equipment or receipt records. Warranty suggestion is not a recorded warranty until accepted and saved. Warning counts are informational.
- Exact navigation/field and screenshot reconciliation remains for Batch 4. Existing source partials and all independent acceptance/release gates remain outstanding.

### Previous partial resolved

- **S5-03 — Core confirmed on follow-up:** [backend/reset_inventory.py:16–90](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/blob/fdc677920bfc52f48f641b3d6d1344c902a6a9b0/backend/reset_inventory.py#L16-L90); [backend/reset_inventory.py:139–161](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/blob/fdc677920bfc52f48f641b3d6d1344c902a6a9b0/backend/reset_inventory.py#L139-L161); [backend/service_desk_schema.py:72–104](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/blob/fdc677920bfc52f48f641b3d6d1344c902a6a9b0/backend/service_desk_schema.py#L72-L104); [backend/service_desk_schema.py:194–218](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/blob/fdc677920bfc52f48f641b3d6d1344c902a6a9b0/backend/service_desk_schema.py#L194-L218). Ticket deletion cascades through messages, attachments, events, closure attempts and per-account message receipts. Operational guards restored before foreign-key verification and commit; rollback on failure; secure_delete enabled. Linked Staff identity and active eligibility, accounts, security, organisation choices and central history retained. Support calendars and report preferences are not listed among reset tables; do not imply these or email configuration are cleared. This resolves the specific prior schema/cascade and transaction boundary, not the deferred native Clean Start or release gate.

### Distinctions for Batch 5 instructions and website copy

- Service Desk is core; equipment module availability and current effective access govern which tickets and equipment can be seen. Never confuse an employee's current Staff linkage with permission to browse IT inventory or historic tickets.
- Assignment is not Work Started. Reopening preserves the ticket and starts a new resolution attempt; closed attempts are not recalculated. No completed closures means a dash, not a measured zero.
- The shared Device confidentiality warning applies before entering ticket details. A newly added Department within the same custody period can see permitted history, but does not receive already-recorded Device notifications. A new custody period does not inherit earlier conversations.
- Period reporting depends on valid installation TZ; ordinary ticket operations and live workload remain available. Preserve the approved installation and DST examples and verify the actual warning in later guide/screenshot review.
- Keep Employee/IT Service Desk separate from Supplier Issues. Next source pass: Email Templates, Company Branding, support mailbox and notification events, then remaining security/Audit Trail/visual refinements and outstanding partials.

### Distinctions for Batch 5 instructions and website copy

- Ten templates, not ten Service Desk events: seven Service Desk and three account/security messages. Explain the exact event and recipient for each, and that there is no notification for an internal note or self-assignment.
- Saving a draft, loading a default, testing and publishing are separate actions. Test emails use fictional details; publishing changes only the selected template. Shared branding is saved independently. Neither branding nor template editing changes SMTP sender settings or security requirements.
- Optional configured IT mailbox receives only the approved new employee-ticket notification. Empty mailbox does not disable Service Desk. Never imply ticket attachment delivery or guaranteed email delivery. Test email success is SMTP submission, not proof of inbox receipt.
- Secure ticket links still require authentication and live access; Department privacy and per-recipient eligibility remain essential. Preserve original verification/recovery proofs and instructions; never use a real code, personal address or production ticket in screenshots.
- SQLite backup includes templates and logo; SMTP credentials are in the separate private data-volume file. Batch 6 must explain complete-volume recovery, not assume a database-only backup restores SMTP. Native acceptance, actual delivery verification and release gates remain outstanding.

### Distinctions for the administrator, employee and migration guides

- Explain sign-in expiry separately from missing permissions and network failures. Recovery, ordinary password change, assisted temporary-password reset and first Administrator setup have distinct flows; do not imply ordinary users can reset administrator passwords or that MFA can be bypassed.
- Protected legacy import is not complete-volume restore or Clean Start. Identical protected Device records retain relationships and revision; conflicts stop replacement rather than deleting protected data. Features switched off do not remove these safeguards.
- Unread counts represent tickets in navigation/scope and replies within a ticket. Only rendered Updates acknowledges eligible messages; do not say opening Overview, polling, or another person's read action clears them. Read receipt and queue behaviour are per account and access scope.
- Audit Trail friendly labels are display-only; historical names must not be reconstructed from present-day records. Original data and authorised JSON export remain intact. Explain that only verified stable identities are grouped; an apparent ID match is not proof of historical continuity. Avoid equating supplier issue and ticket histories with central Audit Trail coverage.
- The remaining six initial entries are S3-08, S6-22, S6-23, S6-25, S6-26 and S6-27 (visual and Manage Permissions refinements). Then reconcile every outstanding partial against final source before declaring Batch 1 complete. Independent security audit and native acceptance remain deferred/outstanding as agreed.

Current source confirms backend summaries but not all-off Audit Trail screen access. Record this limitation for Batch 5 and do not describe an unapproved UI change as required or implemented. If the owner wants all-off Audit Trail navigation, obtain separate approval; no code change is made here.

## Outstanding release gates

No native/manual acceptance resumed; Test 70 next on owner authorisation. Supported upgrade and complete-volume backup/restore final acceptance, further correction reconciliation, independent #443 audit, documentation/screenshot approval, main merge, published image verification and separate release/website authorisations remain outstanding. Staff deletion #471 is future Milestone 10 scope, not a v2.2 feature. No changes to production, main, website or existing published instructions.
